## Notation

Here we summarize the recurring symbols used throughout the survey and
provide a compact guide to their meanings.

|  Symbol |  Description |  Reference |
|----|----|----|
| General agent formalism |  |  |
|  $`\mathcal{A}_{t}`$ |  Agent configuration at iteration $`t`$. |  Sec. [3](#S3 "3 Definitions ‣ Self-Improvements in Modern Agentic Systems: A Survey") |
|  $`\mathcal{A}_{1:t}`$ |  Agent history up to iteration $`t`$. |  Sec. [3](#S3 "3 Definitions ‣ Self-Improvements in Modern Agentic Systems: A Survey") |
|  $`\theta_{t}`$ |  Foundation model parameters. |  Sec. [3](#S3 "3 Definitions ‣ Self-Improvements in Modern Agentic Systems: A Survey") |
|  $`\theta_{1:t}`$ |  Parameter history up to iteration $`t`$. |  Sec. [3](#S3 "3 Definitions ‣ Self-Improvements in Modern Agentic Systems: A Survey") |
|  $`\Sigma_{t}`$ |  Scaffold configuration. |  Sec. [3](#S3 "3 Definitions ‣ Self-Improvements in Modern Agentic Systems: A Survey") |
|  $`\Sigma_{1:t}`$ |  Scaffold history up to iteration $`t`$. |  Sec. [3](#S3 "3 Definitions ‣ Self-Improvements in Modern Agentic Systems: A Survey") |
|  $`p_{t}`$ |  Prompt or instruction component. |  Sec. [3](#S3 "3 Definitions ‣ Self-Improvements in Modern Agentic Systems: A Survey"), Sec. [6.1](#S6.SS1 "6.1 Prompt ‣ 6 Scaffolding Improvement ‣ Self-Improvements in Modern Agentic Systems: A Survey") |
|  $`m_{t}`$ |  Memory component. |  Sec. [3](#S3 "3 Definitions ‣ Self-Improvements in Modern Agentic Systems: A Survey"), Sec. [6.2](#S6.SS2 "6.2 Memory ‣ 6 Scaffolding Improvement ‣ Self-Improvements in Modern Agentic Systems: A Survey") |
|  $`\mathcal{T}_{t}`$ |  Tool component or tool set. |  Sec. [3](#S3 "3 Definitions ‣ Self-Improvements in Modern Agentic Systems: A Survey"), Sec. [6.3](#S6.SS3 "6.3 Tool ‣ 6 Scaffolding Improvement ‣ Self-Improvements in Modern Agentic Systems: A Survey") |
|  $`g_{t}`$ |  Control, routing, or verification logic. |  Sec. [3](#S3 "3 Definitions ‣ Self-Improvements in Modern Agentic Systems: A Survey") |
|  $`\pi_{\theta_{t},\Sigma_{t}}`$ |  Policy induced by model and scaffold. |  Sec. [3](#S3 "3 Definitions ‣ Self-Improvements in Modern Agentic Systems: A Survey") |
|  $`X_{t}`$ |  Input, observation, or task context. |  Sec. [3](#S3 "3 Definitions ‣ Self-Improvements in Modern Agentic Systems: A Survey") |
|  $`A_{t}`$ |  Agent action or output. |  Sec. [3](#S3 "3 Definitions ‣ Self-Improvements in Modern Agentic Systems: A Survey") |
|  $`\mathcal{C}_{t}`$ |  Task or deployment context. |  Sec. [3](#S3 "3 Definitions ‣ Self-Improvements in Modern Agentic Systems: A Survey") |
|  $`\mathcal{E}(\cdot)`$ |  Agent execution procedure. |  Sec. [3](#S3 "3 Definitions ‣ Self-Improvements in Modern Agentic Systems: A Survey") |
|  $`\mathcal{U}`$ |  General self-induced update operator. |  Sec. [3](#S3 "3 Definitions ‣ Self-Improvements in Modern Agentic Systems: A Survey") |
|  $`\mathcal{U}_{\theta}`$ |  Model-parameter update operator. |  Sec. [3](#S3 "3 Definitions ‣ Self-Improvements in Modern Agentic Systems: A Survey") |
|  $`\mathcal{U}_{\Sigma}`$ |  Scaffold update operator. |  Sec. [3](#S3 "3 Definitions ‣ Self-Improvements in Modern Agentic Systems: A Survey") |
|  $`\mathcal{S}_{t}`$ |  Learning or improvement signal. |  Sec. [4](#S4 "4 A Taxonomy of Existing Approaches ‣ Self-Improvements in Modern Agentic Systems: A Survey") |
|  $`\operatorname{\mathtt{IMPROVE}}(\cdot)`$ |  Abstract improvement operator. |  Sec. [4](#S4 "4 A Taxonomy of Existing Approaches ‣ Self-Improvements in Modern Agentic Systems: A Survey") |
|  $`\operatorname{\mathtt{IMPROVE}}_{\theta}(\cdot)`$ |  Foundation-model improvement operator. |  Sec. [5](#S5 "5 Foundation Model Improvement ‣ Self-Improvements in Modern Agentic Systems: A Survey") |
|  $`\operatorname{\mathtt{IMPROVE}}_{\Sigma}(\cdot)`$ |  Scaffold improvement operator. |  Sec. [6](#S6 "6 Scaffolding Improvement ‣ Self-Improvements in Modern Agentic Systems: A Survey") |
| Foundation model improvement |  |  |
|  $`\mathcal{D}_{t}`$ |  Intrinsic generative demonstrations. |  Sec. [5.1](#S5.SS1 "5.1 Intrinsic Generative Demonstrations ‣ 5 Foundation Model Improvement ‣ Self-Improvements in Modern Agentic Systems: A Survey") |
|  $`\mathcal{D}^{\mathrm{gen}}_{t}`$ |  Generated training dataset. |  Sec. [5.1](#S5.SS1 "5.1 Intrinsic Generative Demonstrations ‣ 5 Foundation Model Improvement ‣ Self-Improvements in Modern Agentic Systems: A Survey") |
|  $`(x_{i},y_{i})`$ |  Generated input-output pair. |  Sec. [5.1](#S5.SS1 "5.1 Intrinsic Generative Demonstrations ‣ 5 Foundation Model Improvement ‣ Self-Improvements in Modern Agentic Systems: A Survey") |
|  $`n_{t}`$ |  Number of generated instances. |  Sec. [5.1](#S5.SS1 "5.1 Intrinsic Generative Demonstrations ‣ 5 Foundation Model Improvement ‣ Self-Improvements in Modern Agentic Systems: A Survey") |
|  $`\mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{gen}}`$ |  Agent-induced data distribution. |  Sec. [5.1](#S5.SS1 "5.1 Intrinsic Generative Demonstrations ‣ 5 Foundation Model Improvement ‣ Self-Improvements in Modern Agentic Systems: A Survey") |
|  $`\Phi_{t}`$ |  Data filtering or weighting procedure. |  Sec. [5.1](#S5.SS1 "5.1 Intrinsic Generative Demonstrations ‣ 5 Foundation Model Improvement ‣ Self-Improvements in Modern Agentic Systems: A Survey") |
|  $`\mathcal{L}(\theta;\mathcal{D}_{t})`$ |  Training objective on $`\mathcal{D}_{t}`$. |  Sec. [5.1](#S5.SS1 "5.1 Intrinsic Generative Demonstrations ‣ 5 Foundation Model Improvement ‣ Self-Improvements in Modern Agentic Systems: A Survey") |
|  $`\Omega(\theta,\theta_{0})`$ |  Regularizer around reference parameters. |  Sec. [5.1](#S5.SS1 "5.1 Intrinsic Generative Demonstrations ‣ 5 Foundation Model Improvement ‣ Self-Improvements in Modern Agentic Systems: A Survey") |
|  $`\lambda`$ |  Regularization coefficient. |  Sec. [5.1](#S5.SS1 "5.1 Intrinsic Generative Demonstrations ‣ 5 Foundation Model Improvement ‣ Self-Improvements in Modern Agentic Systems: A Survey") |
|  $`\theta_{0}`$ |  Reference or initial checkpoint. |  Sec. [5.1](#S5.SS1 "5.1 Intrinsic Generative Demonstrations ‣ 5 Foundation Model Improvement ‣ Self-Improvements in Modern Agentic Systems: A Survey") |
|  $`\theta_{t}^{(k)}`$ |  Inner-loop parameter state. |  Sec. [5.1](#S5.SS1 "5.1 Intrinsic Generative Demonstrations ‣ 5 Foundation Model Improvement ‣ Self-Improvements in Modern Agentic Systems: A Survey") |
|  $`\mathcal{B}_{t}^{(k)}`$ |  Minibatch at inner step $`k`$. |  Sec. [5.1](#S5.SS1 "5.1 Intrinsic Generative Demonstrations ‣ 5 Foundation Model Improvement ‣ Self-Improvements in Modern Agentic Systems: A Survey") |
|  $`\eta_{k}`$ |  Inner-loop step size. |  Sec. [5.1](#S5.SS1 "5.1 Intrinsic Generative Demonstrations ‣ 5 Foundation Model Improvement ‣ Self-Improvements in Modern Agentic Systems: A Survey") |
|  $`K_{t}`$ |  Number of inner update steps. |  Sec. [5.1](#S5.SS1 "5.1 Intrinsic Generative Demonstrations ‣ 5 Foundation Model Improvement ‣ Self-Improvements in Modern Agentic Systems: A Survey") |
|  $`\nabla_{\theta}`$ |  Gradient with respect to $`\theta`$. |  Sec. [5.1](#S5.SS1 "5.1 Intrinsic Generative Demonstrations ‣ 5 Foundation Model Improvement ‣ Self-Improvements in Modern Agentic Systems: A Survey") |
|  $`\mathcal{Y}_{t}(x)`$ |  Candidate outputs for input $`x`$. |  Sec. [5.2](#S5.SS2 "5.2 Intrinsic Evaluative Feedback ‣ 5 Foundation Model Improvement ‣ Self-Improvements in Modern Agentic Systems: A Survey") |
|  $`y_{t}^{(k)}`$ |  The $`k`$-th candidate output. |  Sec. [5.2](#S5.SS2 "5.2 Intrinsic Evaluative Feedback ‣ 5 Foundation Model Improvement ‣ Self-Improvements in Modern Agentic Systems: A Survey") |
